Both rulings are in the plan, the review log, and the glossary. No image needed; what you described matched what I had from the recording.

**What changed in the plan**

1. **Pills never ask, never commit.** Press Answer with nothing committed and it just shows the right answer. Press Explanation and it just shows the explanation. No confirm dialog anywhere in the review-controls modes. In filter drills the pick stays uncommitted and the arrow saves it later, tagged `answer_revealed: true`, so the record still knows the answer was seen first. The old assumption ("pill commits the pick first") is withdrawn.
2. **Choice Notes start closed.** Turning Explanation on shows the Argument Breakdown under the stimulus and, under each choice that has a note, a small visible "Explanation ▾" toggle. The student opens one at a time; new question closes them all. Choices with no note get no toggle.

---
name: watch
description: Watch a video (URL or local path). Downloads with yt-dlp, extracts auto-scaled frames with ffmpeg, pulls the transcript from captions (or Whisper API fallback), and hands the result to Claude so it can answer questions about what's in the video.
argument-hint: "<video-url-or-path> [question]"
allowed-tools: Bash, Read, AskUserQuestion
homepage: https://github.com/bradautomates/claude-video
repository: https://github.com/bradautomates/claude-video
author: bradautomates
license: MIT
user-invocable: true
---

# /watch — Claude watches a video

You don't have a video input; this skill gives you one. A Python script downloads the video, extracts frames as JPEGs, gets a timestamped transcript (native captions first, then Whisper API as fallback), and prints frame paths. You then `Read` each frame path to see the images and combine them with the transcript to answer the user.

## Step 0 — Setup preflight (runs every `/watch` invocation, silent on success)

**Python interpreter:** every `python3 ...` command in this skill is for macOS/Linux. On **Windows**, substitute `python` — the `python3` command on Windows is the Microsoft Store stub and will not run the script.

Before every `/watch` run, verify that dependencies and an API key are in place:

```bash
python3 "${CLAUDE_SKILL_DIR}/scripts/setup.py" --check
```

This is a <100ms lookup. On exit 0, the script emits **nothing** — proceed to Step 1 without comment. **Do NOT announce "setup is complete" to the user** — they don't need a status message on every turn. The only acceptable user-visible output from Step 0 is when remediation is required.

On non-zero exit, follow the table:

| Exit | Meaning | Action |
|------|---------|--------|
| `2` | Missing binaries (`ffmpeg` / `ffprobe` / `yt-dlp`) | Run installer |
| `3` | No Whisper API key | Run installer to scaffold `.env`, then ask user for a key |
| `4` | Both missing | Run installer, then ask for a key |

The installer is idempotent — safe to re-run:

```bash
python3 "${CLAUDE_SKILL_DIR}/scripts/setup.py"
```

On macOS with Homebrew, it auto-installs `ffmpeg` and `yt-dlp`. On Linux/Windows, it prints the exact install commands for the user to run. It scaffolds `~/.config/watch/.env` with commented placeholders at `0600` perms, and writes `SETUP_COMPLETE=true` once deps + a key are in place so the next session knows this user has already been through the wizard.

**If an API key is still missing after install:** use `AskUserQuestion` to ask the user whether they have a Groq API key (preferred — cheaper, faster) or an OpenAI key. Then write it into `~/.config/watch/.env` — set the matching `GROQ_API_KEY=...` or `OPENAI_API_KEY=...` line. If they don't want to set up Whisper, proceed with `--no-whisper` and tell them videos without native captions will come back frames-only.

**Structured mode (optional):** `python3 "${CLAUDE_SKILL_DIR}/scripts/setup.py" --json` emits `{status, first_run, missing_binaries, whisper_backend, has_api_key, config_file, platform}` where `status` is one of `ready | needs_install | needs_key | needs_install_and_key`. Use this when you need to branch on specifics (e.g. "is this the user's very first run?" → `first_run: true`).

Within a single session, you can skip Step 0 on follow-up `/watch` calls — once `--check` returned 0, nothing about the environment changes between turns.

## When to use

- User pastes a video URL (YouTube, Vimeo, X, TikTok, Twitch clip, most yt-dlp-supported sites) and asks about it.
- User points at a local video file (`.mp4`, `.mov`, `.mkv`, `.webm`, etc.) and asks about it.
- User types `/watch <url-or-path> [question]`.

## Recommended limits

- **Best accuracy: videos under 10 minutes.** Frame coverage scales inversely with duration.
- **Hard caps: 100 frames total and 2 fps.** Token cost grows with frame count, so the script targets a frame budget by duration (and never exceeds 2 fps ...

### 12. Verify in the browser

Preview server, at 1280, 1024 and 390 widths and with the drawer docked:
- A guided drill: bottom bar is arrows + ovals, no Submit; pick B, press
  Answer → B red, correct green, nothing else; Answer again → colours gone;
  Explanation → breakdown under stimulus, dropdowns open; fold one;
  Explanation off → dropdowns and breakdown gone; right arrow → oval marks
  answered; back arrow → pick and states as left; Reset Response → pick
  cleared, timer running; pick again → the session map shows the new pick
  with a two-entry trail and `answerRevealed: true`; the drawer never
  opened on its own during any of this; End Drill closes out and the
  `attempts` row count for that user + qid grew by exactly one relative to
  a count taken before the drill.
- A filter drill: same, plus arrow persists per question.
- Smart Drill: its own bar unchanged (Submit, Skip, auto-reveal switch);
  pills, breakdown, dropdowns and Reset work; no tutor after a wrong
  Submit.
- Cold cache first open: with an empty React Query cache, press Explanation
  on a fresh question; the slot shows "Loading explanation…" then the
  content, with no layout jump of the stimulus block.
- A sealed run and a full section: selection and navigation unchanged.
- Computed style of the stimulus `div` (font-family, font-size, padding,
  max-width) identical before and after, read with `getComputedStyle`.
- Screenshots for Joshua.

## Out of scope

- Smart Drill's question selection bug and its bottom bar.
- A server-side kill switch on the `tutor-chat` edge function. Edge
  functions do not deploy from a push on this project (memory: prove a
  function deploy with the build marker), so it is its own change; the
  client flag removes every launch point in the product and that is what
  Joshua asked for.
- `TutorChatModal`, token budgeting.
- Sealed runs, practice-test sittings, full-section mode, the classic
  `DrillTopBar` layout: behaviour preserved.
- Reading Comprehension (`RcChoiceDisclosure`) and the Classroom
  `LRQuestionModal`.
- Video explanations: no toggle, no hosting, no claim.
- Authoring the missing explanations.
[/external_agent_tool_result]

## assistant

[external_agent_tool_result]
### 10. Census of questions with no authored explanation

- `scripts/missing-explanations-census.mjs`. Loads the service key the way
  `scripts/db-pull.mjs` does, and **pages every table at 1000 rows with
  `Range` headers until a short page**, the same loop `db-pull.mjs` uses.
  Aborts (non-zero exit, nothing written) on any query error or if a table's
  row count disagrees with a `count=exact` head request.
- Reads the bank from `public/data/PT*-LR.json` (156 files).
- **One predicate, two runtimes, one fixture set.** `hasAuthoredProse` (a
  step or choice-note `body` non-empty after `trim()`) is implemented in
  `useLrExplanation.ts` (replacing the row-count `hasAuthored`) and in the
  script. A vitest spec `explanationProse.fixtures.test.ts` runs **both**
  against the same fixtures (`src/lib/__fixtures__/explanationProse.json`:
  empty, whitespace-only, null, mixed-style, note-only, step-only) by
  importing the `.mjs` predicate directly, so they cannot drift.
- Reasons are exactly what the data proves: `not ingested` (no
  `lr_questions` row for the address) or `no authored prose` (row exists,
  nothing passes the predicate). No "video only" label: nothing in the
  schema records a video; the 14 known video-only questions (PT143, PT146
  per the 2026-08-03 migration) are a subset of "no authored prose", noted
  in the report header.
- Output `ai-os/wiki/explanations/MISSING-EXPLANATIONS.md`: summary counts
  (bank total, ingested, with prose, missing by reason), then one table per
  PrepTest, rows `PT · S · Q · question type · reason`, sorted PT, section,
  question. Run once, committed to the vault, not the app repo.

# Plan: post-question Explanation, LSAT Lab shape, AI Tutor off
_Locked via grill-with-docs on 2026-09-12 by Claude + Joshua. Revised after Codex rounds 1 to 5, re-cut after Joshua's clarification (fixed drills: arrows + bubbles, no Submit), revised again after Codex re-cut rounds 1 and 2 (cap), then Joshua's final rulings: no freeze, Explanation never opens the drawer. Terms per `src/components/drill/CONTEXT.md` (Answer, Explanation, Argument Breakdown, Choice Note, Reset Response, AI Tutor, Answer Navigator, Oval State, Drill Launch, Drill Run Spec)._

## Goal

On the official drill surface (`/drill`), the student reviews a question the
way LSAT Lab shows it. The **Answer** pill and the **Explanation** pill in
the session strip are independent on/off toggles, exactly as in Joshua's
LSAT Lab recording: press to show, press again to hide, and nothing ever
locks the choices. Pressing Explanation shows expanders, not prose: a
chevron on a rule under the stimulus that opens the **Argument Breakdown**,
and a chevron at the right edge of every choice card that opens that
choice's **Choice Note**. Everything starts closed and the student opens
what she wants (Joshua's 2026-09-12 LSAT Lab recording, 13:11). The
drawer's Explanation tab shows the same content but only when the student
opens the drawer herself. A **Reset Response**
control clears the pick. A fixed drill (guided drill, small drill, custom
queue) has no Submit pill: its bottom bar is arrows and ovals, as today,
and nothing shows colour until the student presses Answer. The **AI
Tutor** is off for every student behind one switch, because the Question
Explanation is authored LSAT Lab content already in Postgres
(`lr_explanations`, `lr_explanation_steps`, `lr_explanation_choice_notes`)
and the generated chat was failing on screen. Alongside the code change, a
census of every bank question with no authored prose, keyed PrepTest /
Section / Question.

## How picks work today (the model this plan builds on)

Two pick models exist on the official surface and both stay:

- **List drills** (`isPracticeSetMode`, set at `Drill.tsx:556` for every
  `type-drill` with `selectedQids`: guided drills, assignments, saved sets,
  Intro to LR practice sets, Bootcamp practice). A pick is written straight
  into `session.attempts` with an `answerTrail` and `switchCount`
  (`:1345-1375`); changing the pick is a recorded change of mind; the set
  is persisted at the end (`handleFinishSection` / `handleFinishPracticeSet`).
  The bar's Submit pill is already hidden once a pick exists
  (`primaryVisible={!currentAttempted && !currentUnscored}`, `:4308`).
- **Commit drills** (`type-drill` drawn by `filter`, no `selectedQids`): a
  pick sits in `selectedAnswer` and is persisted per question by
  `commitPendingSelection` when the student presses Next, Previous, an oval
  (`:4297-4326`) or Submit.
- **Smart Drill** (`adaptive`): Submit persists per question; Skip; the
  auto-reveal switch. Joshua reports its question selection is broken; a
  separate job, untouched here.

Both fixed-drill kinds already render arrows + `AnswerOvals` in the bar's
`pager` slot (`:4313`). The ovals mean "which question", never "right or
wrong" (Oval State), and that does not change.

## Scope, classified by Drill Launch kind

`DrillRunSpec` carries no `kind` (ADR 0016: `mode` is not the run kind; six
runs share `type-drill`). Add `launchKind: DrillLaunchBody['kind']` to
`DrillRunSpec`, set in `drillRunSpecFromLaunch`, and classify by it with an
exhaustive switch in a pure helper `src/lib/drillReviewPolicy.ts`:

| launch kind | review policy |
|---|---|
| `guided-drill`, `assignment`, `saved-set`, `questions`, `practice-set`, `bootcamp-day`, `filter` | `fixed` |
| `smart-drill` | `adaptive` |
| `section` | `none` (full section keeps today's opt-in reveal) |
| any kind with `sealed` or a practice-test leg (`ptRun`) | `none` |

`reviewControlsAvailable = policy !== 'none' && isOfficialLayout`.

The **pick model** is a sec...

// "preview" is the pre-submission structural breakdown (Conclusion / Evidence /
// Evaluate / Goal), answer-blind. The other three are the live coaching modes.
export type TutorMode = "preview" | "pre-answer" | "socratic" | "post-answer";

export function resolveTutorMode({
  userAnswer,
  answerRevealed,
}: {
  userAnswer: string | null | undefined;
  answerRevealed: boolean;
}): TutorMode {
  if (answerRevealed) return "post-answer";
  // Before an answer is committed, the Explanation button opens the structural
  // preview rather than streaming coaching.
  return userAnswer ? "socratic" : "preview";
}

export function resolveTutorLaunch({
  tutorModeEnabled,
  correct,
  answerRevealed,
}: {
  tutorModeEnabled: boolean;
  correct: boolean;
  answerRevealed: boolean;
}): { shouldOpen: boolean; mode: TutorMode } {
  return {
    shouldOpen: tutorModeEnabled && !correct,
    mode: resolveTutorMode({ userAnswer: "submitted", answerRevealed }),
  };
}
import * as React from "react";
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";

/**
 * The per-choice explanation layer that sits directly beneath an answer choice
 * in `OfficialQuestionPane`. Replaces the old dark `ExplanationPanel` sheet.
 *
 * The explanation is ALWAYS VISIBLE. It used to sit behind a "Why this is
 * wrong" disclosure, which cost a click per choice and, on a shared screen
 * during teaching, hid the very thing being taught. Reading five choices meant
 * five clicks. The prose is the point of the review screen, so it is set at
 * reading size and shown outright.
 *
 * Two rules carried over from that panel, and tightened to apply per choice
 * rather than per question:
 *  - Never invent content. A missing tag, percentage, or note renders as
 *    absent. A choice with no note gets no disclosure row at all — not an
 *    empty one, not a placeholder.
 *  - The source's nuance lives in the tag chip verbatim ("Too Weak", "Weakens,
 *    if anything"). The disclosure label only says which way the choice went.
 */

export interface InlineChoiceNote {
  /** Wrong-answer type from the source, e.g. "Out of Scope". */
  tag?: string | null;
  /** Percent of students who picked this choice. */
  studentPercent?: number | null;
  /** The choice's explanation prose. Absent for letters the source skipped. */
  note?: string | null;
}

/** Per-letter inline content for one question. A letter may legitimately be absent. */
export type InlineChoiceNotes = Record<string, InlineChoiceNote>;

interface Props {
  letter: string;
  isCorrect: boolean;
  /** The student's own pick, marked so they can find it fast. */
  isSelected: boolean;
  content: InlineChoiceNote;
}

/** True when there is nothing real to render for this letter. */
export function isInlineChoiceEmpty(content: InlineChoiceNote | undefined, isSelected: boolean) {
  if (isSelected) return false;
  if (!content) return true;
  return !content.tag && content.studentPercent == null && !content.note;
}

export default function InlineChoiceExplanation({
  letter,
  isCorrect,
  isSelected,
  content,
}: Props) {
  const { tag, studentPercent, note } = content;
  const hasNote = typeof note === "string" && note.trim().length > 0;

  if (isInlineChoiceEmpty(content, isSelected)) return null;

  return (
    <div className="mt-2.5">
      {/* Tag, percentage, and the student's own pick. Any of these may be absent. */}
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 text-[12px]">
        {tag && (
          <span
            className={cn(
              "rounded-full px-2 py-0.5 text-[11px] font-semibold",
              isCorrect ? "bg-green-600 text-white" : "bg-slate-200 text-slate-700",
            )}
          >
            {tag}
          </span>
        )}
        {studentPercent != null && (
          <span className="text-slate-500 tabular-nums">{studentPercent}% of students</span>
        )}
        {isSelected && (
          <span className="rounded-full border border-slate-300 px-2 py-0.5 text-[11px] font-semibol...
[/external_agent_tool_result]

import { DrillLaunchError, type DrillLaunch, type PtLegRef } from '@/lib/drillLaunch';

export interface DrillRunSpec {
  mode?: DrillMode;
  config?: FullSectionConfig | TypeDrillConfig;
  /** Heading shown while the drill runs. */
  practicingLabel?: string;
  /** Where the runner's exit sends the student. Always app-relative. */
  returnTo?: string;
  /** True when the runner is launched from a Guided Drill. */
  isGuidedDrill?: boolean;
  guidedDurationMinutes?: number;
  /**
   * Presentation hint, independent of `mode`. When 'smart', the runner renders
   * the adaptive Smart-Drill chrome even for a fixed-queue type-drill.
   */
  present?: 'smart';
  /**
   * Seals the run: Answer and Explanation are not rendered at all, for the
   * whole session, for every question. A launch property and not a student
   * setting, because the person who assigns the section decides.
   */
  sealed?: boolean;
  /** Open on this question rather than the first. */
  initialIndex?: number;

  // ── Which stored row this run belongs to ────────────────────────────────
  // Previously read straight off the query string by the runner, in a second
  // grammar that only agreed with the codec by coincidence.
  /** A classroom homework assignment. */
  assignmentId?: string;
  guidedSetId?: string;
  guidedDrillId?: string;
  guidedAssignmentId?: string;
  /** Intro to LR practice set: what it is, and which pool. */
  practiceSource?: string;
  practiceSlug?: string;
  /** Names the Run Record for a bootcamp day, so a re-draw cannot orphan it. */
  resumeIdentity?: DrillResumeIdentity;
  /** Which practice-test run and leg this section closes out. */
  ptRun?: PtLegRef;
}

export interface DrillTimerConfig {
  mode: 'countdown' | 'stopwatch';
  durationMs?: number;
}

function typeDrillConfig(qids: string[]): TypeDrillConfig {
  return {
    qtypes: [],
    difficulties: [],
    pts: [],
    count: qids.length,
    selectedQids: qids,
  };
}

const QID_PATTERN = /^PT(\d+)-S(\d+)-Q\d+$/;

/**
 * A homework set built from one real section — every qid comes from the same
 * PT and section number, the shape `assign.ts` and the admin set builder both
 * produce today. Such a set is a section wearing a homework row, not a mixed
 * question list, so it runs under a real countdown instead of the untimed
 * stopwatch every other homework set gets. A set that mixes sections (or PTs)
 * returns null and keeps the untimed behaviour, because there is no one clock
 * that honestly times a blend.
 */
export function singleSectionOf(qids: string[]): { pt: number; section: number } | null {
  if (qids.length === 0) return null;
  let pt: number | null = null;
  let section: number | null = null;
  for (const qid of qids) {
    const match = QID_PATTERN.exec(qid);
    if (!match) return null;
    const qPt = Number(match[1]);
    const qSection = Number(match[2]);
    if (pt === null) {
      pt = qPt;
      section = qSection;
    } else if (qPt !== pt || qSection !== section) {
      return null;
    }
  }
  return pt !== null && section !== null ? { pt, section } : null;
}

    present: options.present,
    initialIndex: options.initialIndex,
  };

    case 'smart-drill':
      return { ...base, mode: 'adaptive', sealed: false };

    case 'section': {

import type { DrillLaunchBody } from '@/lib/drillLaunch';
import type { DrillRunSpec } from '@/lib/drillRunSpec';

/**
 * How the official drill surface lets a student review a question.
 *
 *  - `fixed`: a fixed queue (guided drill, assignment, saved set, custom
 *    questions, practice set, bootcamp day, filter draw). Answer and
 *    Explanation are independent toggles, no Submit pill, nothing locks.
 *  - `adaptive`: Smart Drill. Same pills, but its own bar (Submit, Skip, the
 *    auto-reveal switch) stays.
 *  - `none`: a full section, a sealed run, or a practice-test leg. Today's
 *    coupled reveal, untouched.
 *
 * A pure function of the resolved spec, so an early render can never
 * misclassify a run the way the mutable `isPracticeSetMode` state could.
 */
export type ReviewPolicy = 'fixed' | 'adaptive' | 'none';

/**
 * Which pick model a run uses. `list`: a pick is written straight into
 * `session.attempts` and the set is persisted at the end. `commit`: a pick
 * sits in `selectedAnswer` and is persisted per question on the way out.
 * Independent of the review policy, because a `questions` launch can arrive as
 * `natural-drill` and never sets a selected-qid list.
 */
export type PickModel = 'list' | 'commit';

function policyForKind(kind: DrillLaunchBody['kind']): ReviewPolicy {
  switch (kind) {
    case 'guided-drill':
    case 'assignment':
    case 'saved-set':
    case 'questions':
    case 'practice-set':
    case 'bootcamp-day':
    case 'filter':
      return 'fixed';
    case 'smart-drill':
      return 'adaptive';
    case 'section':
      return 'none';
    default: {
      const never: never = kind;
      return never;
    }
  }
}

export function reviewPolicyForRun(spec: DrillRunSpec): ReviewPolicy {
  if (spec.sealed || spec.ptRun) return 'none';
  if (!spec.launchKind) return 'none';
  return policyForKind(spec.launchKind);
}

/** The exact condition the runner uses to enter the in-memory-attempt model. */
export function hasSelectedQidList(spec: DrillRunSpec): boolean {
  if (spec.mode !== 'type-drill') return false;
  const config = spec.config as { selectedQids?: string[] } | undefined;
  return !!config?.selectedQids && config.selectedQids.length > 0;
}

export function pickModelForRun(spec: DrillRunSpec): PickModel {
  return hasSelectedQidList(spec) ? 'list' : 'commit';
}
EOF

function stripDataArtifacts(text: string): string {
  return text

    .trim();
}

export interface DrillExplanationContext {

import { fireEvent, render, screen } from "@testing-library/react";
import { afterEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import OfficialQuestionPane from "./OfficialQuestionPane";
import { choiceSection, type Highlight } from "@/lib/highlightUtils";

/** The element that carries a choice's colour classes: the text block itself. */
function choiceText(name: RegExp): HTMLElement {
  const el = screen.getByText(name).closest("[id^='choice-']");
  if (!(el instanceof HTMLElement)) throw new Error(`no choice block for ${name}`);
  return el;
}

/** Fake a live selection of `text` so the pane sees a drag, not a click. */
function selectText(text: string) {
  const node = screen.getByText(text).firstChild as Text;
  const range = document.createRange();
  range.setStart(node, 0);
  range.setEnd(node, text.length);
  const selection = window.getSelection()!;
  selection.removeAllRanges();
  selection.addRange(range);
}

afterEach(() => {
  window.getSelection()?.removeAllRanges();
});

const question = {
  qid: "q-26",
  qnum: 26,
  stimulus:
    "A recent poll showed that almost half of the city's residents believe that Mayor Walker is guilty of ethics violations.",
  questionStem:
    "Which one of the following, if true, most helps to explain the surprising fact stated above?",
  answerChoices: {
    A: "Almost all of the people who believe that Walker is guilty of ethics violations had thought his performance was poor.",
    B: "There has been an increase in ratings for Walker's political opponents.",
  },
};

function renderPane(questionOverride = question) {
  return render(
    <OfficialQuestionPane
      question={questionOverride}
      displayNumber={26}
      selectedAnswer="A"
      eliminatedAnswers={new Set()}
      isFlagged={false}
      answerRevealed
      correctAnswer="A"
      highlights={[]}
      highlightMode="none"
      onAnswerSelect={vi.fn()}
      onEliminate={vi.fn()}
      onToggleFlag={vi.fn()}
      onTextSelection={vi.fn()}
      onHighlightClick={vi.fn()}
    />,
  );
}

/**
 * The one predicate for "this question has an authored written explanation".
 *
 * Shared by the app (`src/hooks/useLrExplanation.ts` re-implements it in TS)
 * and by `scripts/missing-explanations-census.mjs`. Both are run against the
 * same fixtures in `src/lib/explanationProse.fixtures.test.ts`, so the census
 * cannot count a question one way while the drill surface treats it another.
 *
 * A style has prose when at least one breakdown step or one choice note has a
 * body that is non-empty after trim(). Tags and student percentages never
 * count: a question can have every choice tagged and still have nothing to
 * read.
 *
 * @typedef {{ body?: string | null }} ProseRow
 * @typedef {{ steps?: ProseRow[] | null, notes?: ProseRow[] | null }} ProseStyle
 */

/** @param {unknown} body */
export function isProse(body) {
  return typeof body === 'string' && body.trim().length > 0;
}

/** @param {ProseStyle[] | null | undefined} styles */
export function hasAuthoredProse(styles) {
  if (!Array.isArray(styles)) return false;
  return styles.some(
    (style) =>
      (style?.steps ?? []).some((row) => isProse(row?.body)) ||
      (style?.notes ?? []).some((row) => isProse(row?.body)),
  );
}
EOF

import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { hasAuthoredProse as scriptPredicate } from '../../scripts/explanation-prose.mjs';
import { hasAuthoredProse as appPredicate } from '@/hooks/useLrExplanation';
import fixtures from './__fixtures__/explanationProse.json';

/**
 * One predicate, two runtimes, one fixture set. The census script and the
 * drill surface must agree on which questions have a written explanation, so
 * both implementations run the same cases here.
 */
describe('hasAuthoredProse', () => {
  it.each(fixtures)('$name (app)', ({ styles, expected }) => {
    expect(appPredicate(styles)).toBe(expected);
  });

  it.each(fixtures)('$name (census script)', ({ styles, expected }) => {
    expect(scriptPredicate(styles)).toBe(expected);
  });
});

import io
p="src/hooks/useLrExplanation.ts"
s=io.open(p,encoding="utf-8").read()
reps=[
(""" * Coverage is deliberately partial. Roughly 350 of the ~3,770 ingested
 * questions have no authored prose at all — LSAT Lab published only a video
 * for them. `hasAuthored` is the flag callers should branch on; that is the
 * signal for falling back to the AI tutor.
 */""",
""" * Coverage is deliberately partial. Roughly 350 of the ~3,770 ingested
 * questions have no authored prose at all — LSAT Lab published only a video
 * for them. `hasAuthored` is the flag callers should branch on. It is computed
 * by `hasAuthoredProse`, the same predicate `scripts/missing-explanations-census.mjs`
 * uses; `src/lib/explanationProse.fixtures.test.ts` holds both to one fixture set.
 */"""),
("""export interface LrExplanationQuery {""",
"""/** The shape both runtimes' predicate reads: a list of styles, each with step and note bodies. */
export interface ProseStyle {
  steps?: Array<{ body?: string | null }> | null;
  notes?: Array<{ body?: string | null }> | null;
}

function isProse(body: unknown): boolean {
  return typeof body === 'string' && body.trim().length > 0;
}

/**
 * True when at least one style carries real prose: a breakdown step or a
 * per-choice note whose body is non-empty after trim(). Tags and percentages
 * never count. Mirrors `scripts/explanation-prose.mjs` exactly.
 */
export function hasAuthoredProse(styles: ProseStyle[] | null | undefined): boolean {
  if (!Array.isArray(styles)) return false;
  return styles.some(
    (style) =>
      (style?.steps ?? []).some((row) => isProse(row?.body)) ||
      (style?.notes ?? []).some((row) => isProse(row?.body)),
  );
}

/**
 * Which steps to show for the active style. Where a non-Standard style carries
 * no steps, the source said "(same as Standard)" and did not repeat the text
 * (the ingest stored zero step rows for that style rather than the literal
 * phrase; checked 2026-09-12 against `fetchLrExplanation`, which only ever
 * builds steps from `lr_explanation_steps` rows). Rendering nothing would
 * misrepresent that, so Standard's breakdown is borrowed and labelled. The
 * absence only ever means "identical to Standard", never "none exists".
 */
export function resolveBreakdownSteps(
  data: LrExplanationData | null | undefined,
  activeStyle: LrStyleKey | null,
): { current: LrStyle | undefined; steps: LrStep[]; borrowsStandard: boolean } {
  const styles = data?.styles ?? [];
  const current = styles.find((s) => s.style === activeStyle) ?? styles[0];
  const standardSteps = styles.find((s) => s.style === 'standard')?.steps ?? [];
  const borrowsStandard =
    current != null &&
    current.style !== 'standard' &&
    current.steps.length === 0 &&
    standardSteps.length > 0;
  return { current, steps: borrowsStandard ? standardSteps : current?.steps ?? [], borrowsStandard };
}

export interface LrExplanationQuery {"""),
("""  const hasAuthored = styles.some(
    (s) => s.steps.length > 0 || Object.keys(s.notesByLetter).length > 0,
  );""",
"""  const hasAuthored = hasAuthoredProse(
    styles.map((s) => ({
      steps: s.steps,
      notes: Object.values(s.notesByLetter).map((body) => ({ body })),
    })),
  );"""),
]

("""  revealed,
  sealed,

}: DrillExplanationContext) {

  const current = styles.find((s) => s.style === activeStyle) ?? styles[0];

  const standardSteps = styles.find((s) => s.style === "standard")?.steps ?? [];
  const borrowsStandard =
    current != null &&
    current.style !== "standard" &&
    current.steps.length === 0 &&
    standardSteps.length > 0;
  const steps = borrowsStandard ? standardSteps : current?.steps ?? [];

  if (!revealed) {
    return (
      <Body>
        <p className="text-[13px] text-slate-400">
          {sealed
            ? 'Explanations open after the section is scored.'

"""  revealed,
  sealed,
  explanationPill,
  activeStyle,
  onStyleChange,
  onAskTutor,
}: DrillExplanationContext) {

  const { current, steps, borrowsStandard } = resolveBreakdownSteps(data, activeStyle);

    return (

            : explanationPill

  }"""),

  const handleAnswerSelect = (answer: string) => {
    if (!isPracticeSetMode && !isRetryAfterWrong && answerLocked) return;
    if (!tutorMode && showSolution) return;
    
    if (selectedAnswer === answer) {
      setSelectedAnswer('');
      if ((session?.mode === 'full-section' || isPracticeSetMode) && currentQuestion) {
        const newAttempts = new Map(session.attempts);
        newAttempts.delete(currentQuestion.qid);
        setSession({ ...session, attempts: newAttempts });
        
        if (isPracticeSetMode) {
          questionTimer.current.recordAnswer(currentQuestion.qid, '');
        }
      }
    } else {
      setSelectedAnswer(answer);
      if ((session?.mode === 'full-section' || isPracticeSetMode) && currentQuestion) {
        const newAttempts = new Map(session.attempts);
        const existingAttempt = newAttempts.get(currentQuestion.qid);
        // When this choice was made, measured from the start of the question.
        const atMs = Math.max(
          0,
          trailBaseMs.current + Math.floor(performance.now() - questionStartTime),
        );
        const answerTrail = [...(existingAttempt?.answerTrail ?? []), { answer, atMs }];

        newAttempts.set(currentQuestion.qid, {
          selectedAnswer: answer,
          correct: answer === currentQuestion.correctAnswer,
          timeMs: isPracticeSetMode
            ? questionTimer.current.getTotalTime(currentQuestion.qid)
            : Math.floor(performance.now() - questionStartTime),
          timestamp: Date.now(),
          confidence: existingAttempt?.confidence || null,
          reviewDone: false,
          answerRevealed: existingAttempt?.answerRevealed || false,
          answerTrail,
          // Every entry after the first is a change of mind. Derived here so
          // the count and the trail can never disagree.
          switchCount: Math.max(0, answerTrail.length - 1),
        });
        setSession({ ...session, attempts: newAttempts });
        
        if (isPracticeSetMode) {
          questionTimer.current.recordAnswer(currentQuestion.qid, answer);
        }
      }
    }
  };

  const handleEliminateAnswer = (key: string) => {
    if (!currentQuestion) return;
    const qid = currentQuestion.qid;
    setEliminatedByQid(prev => {
      const next = new Map(prev);
      const current = new Set(prev.get(qid) ?? []);
      if (current.has(key)) {
        current.delete(key);
      } else {
        current.add(key);
        if (selectedAnswer === key) {
          setSelectedAnswer('');
          if ((session?.mode === 'full-section' || isPracticeSetMode) && session) {
            const newAttempts = new Map(session.attempts);
            newAttempts.delete(qid);
            setSession({ ...session, attempts: newAttempts });
          }
        }
      }
      next.set(qid, current);
      return next;
    });
  };

        toast.error("Couldn't save your answer. Check your connection and try the next question.");

    await flushReplay(currentQuestion.qid, session.mode);

      correct,
      time_ms: timeMs,
      qtype: currentQuestion.qtype,
      difficulty: currentQuestion.difficulty,
      timestamp: new Date(),
      reasoningType: currentQuestion.reasoningType ?? [],
      questionFeatures: currentQuestion.questionFeatures ?? [],
    });

    // Track question usage
    if (classId && session?.mode) {
      QuestionPoolService.markQuestionSeen(currentQuestion.qid, classId, session.mode);
    }

    setSession({ ...session, attempts: newAttempts });
    if (!options?.advanceAfterSubmit) {
      setShowSolution(true);
    }
    return newAttempts;
  };

[external_agent_tool_result]
  const saveAttemptToDatabase = async (attemptData: {
    qid: string;
    correct: boolean;
    time_ms: number;
    qtype: string;
    level: number;
    confidence: number | null;
    mode: DrillMode;
    selected_answer?: string;
  }): Promise<boolean> => {
    const question = questionBank.getQuestion(attemptData.qid);
    if (!question) {
      throw new Error(`saveAttemptToDatabase: unknown qid ${attemptData.qid}`);
    }

    if (!classId && classIdLoading) {
      console.warn('saveAttemptToDatabase called before classId resolved, skipping');
      return false;
    }
    const resolvedClassId = classId || user?.id || '';
    if (!resolvedClassId) {
      throw new Error(
        `saveAttemptToDatabase: no class_id and no user.id for qid ${attemptData.qid}`,
      );
    }
    try {
      const { error } = await supabase.from('attempts').insert({
        user_id: user?.id,
        class_id: resolvedClassId,
        qid: attemptData.qid,
        pt: question.pt,
        section: question.section,
        qnum: question.qnum,
        qtype: attemptData.qtype,
        level: attemptData.level,
        correct: attemptData.correct,
        time_ms: attemptData.time_ms,
        confidence: attemptData.confidence,
        mode: attemptData.mode,
        selected_answer: attemptData.selected_answer || null,
        timestamp_iso: new Date().toISOString(),
      });

      if (error) {
        console.error('[drill:saveAttempt] supabase insert failed', {
          userId: user?.id,
          qid: attemptData.qid,
          mode: attemptData.mode,
          error,
        });
        throw new Error(`saveAttemptToDatabase: insert failed (${error.message})`);
      }
      return true;
    } catch (err) {
      console.error('[drill:saveAttempt] threw', {
        userId: user?.id,
        qid: attemptData.qid,
        mode: attemptData.mode,
        err,
      });
      throw err;
    }
  };

  // UI-level helper for the per-question submit handlers. Wraps the throwing
  // service so existing `if (!saved)` toast logic keeps working — the error is
  // logged at the service AND visible to the user via the caller's toast. This
  // does NOT silently swallow: failures are logged + surfaced through the
  // boolean return that callers already act on.

  /**
   * How much of the section clock has been spent, right now.
   *
   * A countdown reports what is left, so the spend is the budget minus that; a
   * stopwatch already reports the spend. Null when there is no clock at all,
   * which is the difference between "no time used" and "no time to use".
   */
  const sectionElapsedMs = React.useCallback((): number | undefined => {
    if (!timer) return undefined;
    if (timer.mode === 'stopwatch') return Math.max(0, Math.round(timer.ms));
    const total = sectionBudgetMs(session?.fullSectionConfig?.timer);
    if (!total) return undefined;
    return Math.max(0, Math.round(total - timer.ms));
  }, [timer, session?.fullSectionConfig?.timer]);

  const drillResumeSnapshot = React.useCallback(
    (source: DrillSession | null): PracticeSessionSnapshot | null => {

        questionQueue: source.questionQueue,

  const openTutorForCurrent = (tab?: 'coach' | 'breakdown' | 'notes') => {
    if (!currentQuestion) return;
    setTutorQuestionSnapshot(currentQuestion);
    setTutorUserAnswerSnapshot(selectedAnswer || '');
    setTutorChatMode(resolveTutorMode({
      userAnswer: selectedAnswer,
      answerRevealed: !!showAnswerRevealed.get(currentQuestion.qid),
    }));
    setTutorInitialTab(tab);
    setTutorChatOpen(true);
  };

  // Reveal the correct answer in place, without opening any panel. Mirrors the
  // auto-reveal toggle's effect for a student who left it off.
  const revealAnswerForCurrent = () => {
    if (!currentQuestion) return;
    setShowAnswerRevealed(prev => new Map(prev).set(currentQuestion.qid, true));
  };

  // Revealing is a toggle, not a one-way door. Pressing Answer or Explanation
  // again puts the question back the way it was: correct letter hidden, the
  // per-choice explanations collapsed, the choices live again. Teaching off a
  // built set needs exactly this — showing the answer used to spend the
  // question for good.
  //
  // Only the view is undone. An attempt already written keeps
  // `answerRevealed: true`, because that record is what happened, not what is
  // currently on screen.
  const hideAnswerForCurrent = () => {
    if (!currentQuestion) return;
    const qid = currentQuestion.qid;
    setShowAnswerRevealed(prev => {
      if (!prev.has(qid)) return prev;
      const next = new Map(prev);
      next.delete(qid);
      return next;
    });
    // The peek is forgiven along with the reveal: `unscoredQids` only gates
    // this runner's chrome, so leaving it set would strand the question on
    // Next with no way back to Submit.

describe("OfficialQuestionPane", () => {
  it("keeps the revealed correct answer readable on the official white surface", () => {
    const { container } = renderPane();

    const root = container.firstElementChild;
    expect(root).toHaveClass("bg-white");
    expect(root).not.toHaveClass("bg-bg-shadow");

    // The choice text is not a button any more (a button cannot be
    // drag-selected, so it could not be highlighted); find it by its text.
    const correctAnswerText = choiceText(/Almost all of the people who believe that Walker is guilty/i);
    expect(correctAnswerText).toHaveClass("text-green-900");
    expect(correctAnswerText).not.toHaveClass("text-green-50");
  });

  it("renders multi-word speaker labels as separate dialogue turns", () => {
    const { container } = renderPane({
      ...question,
      stimulus:
        "Figorian Wildlife Commission: First speaker text.\n\nFigorian Development Commission: Second speaker text.",
    });

    expect(screen.getByText("Figorian Wildlife Commission:")).toHaveClass("font-bold");
    expect(screen.getByText("Figorian Development Commission:")).toHaveClass("font-bold");
    expect(container.querySelector(".space-y-4")?.children).toHaveLength(2);
  });

  it("does not treat a mid-argument clause ending in a colon as a speaker", () => {
    // Even if a stray break lands before a sentence clause, the renderer must not
    // bold it as a turn (regression: PT118 S3 Q7-8 showed three bold speakers).
    const { container } = renderPane({
      ...question,
      stimulus:
        "Figorian Wildlife Commission: First speaker text.\n\nTo protect wildlife we must regulate such development in Figoria: more text.\n\nFigorian Development Commission: Second speaker text.",
    });

    expect(
      screen.queryByText(
        "To protect wildlife we must regulate such development in Figoria:",
      ),
    ).not.toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(container.querySelector(".space-y-4")).not.toBeInTheDocument();
  });
});

describe("OfficialQuestionPane — revealing an answer", () => {
  function renderRevealed(props: Record<string, unknown> = {}) {
    return render(
      <OfficialQuestionPane
        question={question}
        displayNumber={26}
        selectedAnswer="B"
        eliminatedAnswers={new Set()}
        isFlagged={false}
        answerRevealed
        correctAnswer="A"
        highlights={[]}
        highlightMode="none"
        onAnswerSelect={vi.fn()}
        onEliminate={vi.fn()}
        onToggleFlag={vi.fn()}
        onTextSelection={vi.fn()}
        onHighlightClick={vi.fn()}
        {...props}
      />,
    );
  }

  it("marks the choice they actually took, not only the one that was right", () => {
    renderRevealed();

    // B was chosen and is wrong; saying "A is correct" without saying "and you
    // took B" is only half of what a reveal is for.
    expect(choiceText(/There has been an increase in ratings/i)).toHaveClass("text-red-900");
  });

  it("does not leave the verdict to colour alone", () => {
    // ADR 0001: feedback must always carry an icon and a label, so a student who
    // cannot separate the green from the red still gets the answer.

#!/usr/bin/env node
/**
 * db-pull.mjs — read a table out of the live database, and only read it.
 *
 * Read-only by construction: it speaks PostgREST over HTTP GET, so there is no
 * statement to get wrong and no way for this to write, drop or migrate
 * anything. That is the point. Arbitrary SQL against production is available
 * through the Lovable MCP connector, but that tool cannot distinguish a SELECT
 * from a DROP, so it stays gated and deliberately awkward. Pulling rows for an
 * audit should not require opening that door.
 *
 * Needs a service-role key, because RLS hides every row from the anon key and
 * an empty result is indistinguishable from an empty table. Set it in
 * ~/ai-os/.env (which is git-ignored and already holds every other credential):
 *
 *   SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY=<the service_role key from the Supabase dashboard,
 *                              Project Settings -> API. It is a long JWT
 *                              starting "eyJ", not the short anon key.>
 *
 * Usage:
 *   node scripts/db-pull.mjs attempts > /tmp/attempts.json
 *   node scripts/db-pull.mjs attempts id,user_id,pt,section,qnum,selected_answer,correct
 *
 * Pairs with the grading auditor:
 *   node scripts/db-pull.mjs attempts > /tmp/attempts.json
 *   node scripts/audit-attempt-grading.mjs /tmp/attempts.json
 */
import fs from 'node:fs';
import path from 'node:path';
import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';

const __dirname = path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));
const REPO = path.resolve(__dirname, '..');

function readEnv(file) {
  const out = {};
  if (!fs.existsSync(file)) return out;
  for (const line of fs.readFileSync(file, 'utf8').split('\n')) {
    const t = line.trim();
    if (!t || t.startsWith('#')) continue;
    const i = t.indexOf('=');
    if (i < 0) continue;
    out[t.slice(0, i).trim()] = t.slice(i + 1).trim().replace(/^["']|["']$/g, '');
  }
  return out;
}

const env = {
  ...readEnv(path.resolve(REPO, '../../.env')),
  ...readEnv(path.join(REPO, '.env')),
};

const table = process.argv[2];
const columns = process.argv[3] ?? '*';
if (!table) {
  console.error('usage: node scripts/db-pull.mjs <table> [columns]');
  process.exit(2);
}

const url = env.VITE_SUPABASE_URL;
const key = env.SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY;

if (!url) {
  console.error('db-pull: VITE_SUPABASE_URL is not set in projects/lsatu/.env');
  process.exit(2);
}
if (!key) {
  console.error(
    'db-pull: SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY is not set.\n' +
      '  Add it to ~/ai-os/.env (git-ignored). Supabase dashboard -> Project Settings -> API\n' +
      '  -> service_role. Without it RLS returns zero rows and an empty table and a hidden\n' +
      '  one look identical, so this refuses to guess rather than reporting a false empty.',
  );
  process.exit(2);
}

// Page through: PostgREST caps a response, and a silently truncated pull is the
// failure mode that makes an audit say "all clear" over rows it never saw.
const PAGE = 1000;
const rows = [];
for (let from = 0; ; from += PAGE) {
  const res = await fetch(`${url}/rest/v1/${table}?select=${encodeURIComponent(columns)}`, {
    headers: {
      apikey: [REDACTED],
      Authorization: [REDACTED] ${key}`,
      Range: `${from}-${from + PAGE - 1}`,
      Prefer: 'count=exact',
    },
  });
  if (!res.ok) {
    console.error(`db-pull: ${res.status} ${res.statusText}\n${(await res.text()).slice(0, 400)}`);
    process.exit(1);
  }
  const batch = await res.json();
  rows.push(...batch);
  if (batch.length < PAGE) break;
}

process.stderr.write(`db-pull: ${rows.length} row(s) from ${table}\n`);
process.stdout.write(JSON.stringify({ rows }, null, 2));

              [questions[i], questions[j]] = [questions[j], questions[i]];

/** The stored run for this drill, or null when there is nothing to resume. */
export async function loadDrillResumeSession(
  userId: string,
  key: string | null,
  legacyKey?: string | null,
): Promise<DrillResumeSession | null> {
  if (!userId || !key) return null;
  const current = await loadPracticeSession(userId, key);
  if (current || !legacyKey || legacyKey === key) return current;
  return loadPracticeSession(userId, legacyKey);
}

/** Write where the student is right now (local-first, debounced cloud upsert). */
export function saveDrillResumeSnapshot(
  userId: string,
  key: string | null,
  snapshot: DrillResumeSnapshot,
): Promise<void> {
  if (!userId || !key) return Promise.resolve();
  return savePracticeSessionSnapshot(userId, { slug: key, completionId: key }, snapshot);
}

/**
 * Mark the run finished. Nothing is deleted — the queue and every answer stay
 * in the row for review — it simply stops being offered as a resume, so the
 * next launch of the same drill draws fresh questions.
 */
export function completeDrillResumeSession(
  userId: string,
  key: string | null,
  snapshot: DrillResumeSnapshot,
): Promise<void> {
  if (!userId || !key) return Promise.resolve();
  return completePracticeSession(userId, { slug: key, completionId: key }, snapshot);
}

/**
 * Is this stored row worth resuming?
 *
 * Unlike a guided drill, these runs are drawn fresh on every launch, so there
 * is no fixed set to compare against — the stored queue IS the run. It is
 * resumable when it is still in progress and the student actually answered
 * something; a row with no answers has nothing to restore and would only
 * freeze a stale shuffle in place.
 */
export function isResumableDrillSession(stored: DrillResumeSession | null): boolean {
  return (
    !!stored &&
    stored.status === 'in_progress' &&
    stored.question_queue.length > 0 &&
    stored.attempts.length > 0
  );

    [sectionElapsedMs],
  );

  /**
   * The clock keeps running between answers, and a student can sit on one
   * question for five minutes. Snapshots written only on answer changes would
   * hand those five minutes back on resume, so a timed section also saves on a
   * slow heartbeat. Held in a ref because the snapshot function changes on
   * every tick and would otherwise restart the interval each time.
   */
  const snapshotRef = React.useRef(drillResumeSnapshot);
  snapshotRef.current = drillResumeSnapshot;
  React.useEffect(() => {
    if (!genericResumeKey || !user || !session || !timer) return;
    if (genericFinalizedRef.current) return;
    if (session.mode !== 'full-section') return;
    const id = window.setInterval(() => {
      const snapshot = snapshotRef.current(session);
      if (!snapshot || snapshot.attempts.length === 0) return;
      void saveDrillResumeSnapshot(user.id, genericResumeKey, snapshot).catch((err) => {
        console.error('[drill:resume:heartbeat] resume write failed', { err });
      });
    }, 20_000);
    return () => window.clearInterval(id);
  }, [genericResumeKey, user, session, timer]);

  React.useEffect(() => {
    if (!isMcPractice || !mcPracticePool || !user || !session) return;
    if (mcPracticeFinalizedRef.current) return;
    if (session.questionQueue.length === 0) return;
    const snapshot = drillResumeSnapshot(session);
    if (!snapshot) return;
    void savePracticeSessionSnapshot(user.id, mcPracticePool, snapshot).catch((err) => {
      // Non-fatal: the drill still works, they just lose the resume point.
      console.error('[drill:mc-practice:snapshot] resume write failed', {
        userId: user.id,
        slug: mcPracticePool.slug,
        err,
      });
    });
  }, [session, isMcPractice, mcPracticePool, user, drillResumeSnapshot]);

  // Same snapshot on every answer and every question move for guided drills, so
  // the student's place survives a tab close, a sign-out, or another device —
  // none of which a URL param can carry.
  React.useEffect(() => {
    if (!guidedSessionKey || !user || !session) return;
    if (guidedFinalizedRef.current) return;
    if (session.questionQueue.length === 0) return;
    const snapshot = drillResumeSnapshot(session);
    if (!snapshot) return;
    void saveGuidedDrillSnapshot(user.id, guidedSessionKey, snapshot).catch((err) => {
      // Non-fatal: the drill still works, they just lose the resume point.
      console.error('[drill:guided:snapshot] resume write failed', {
        userId: user.id,
        key: guidedSessionKey,
        err,
      });
    });
  }, [session, guidedSessionKey, user, drillResumeSnapshot]);

  // And the same again for every other drill surface. Only runs with at least
  // one answered question are written: an untouched shuffle is not progress,
  // and storing it would freeze a stale question order in place.
  React.useEffect(() => {
    if (!genericResumeKey || !user || !session) return;
    if (genericFinalizedRef.current) return;
    if (session.questionQueue.length === 0) return;
    const snapshot = drillResumeSnapshot(session);
    if (!snapshot || snapshot.attempts.length === 0) return;
    void saveDrillResumeSnapshot(user.id, genericResumeKey, snapshot).catch((err) => {
      // Non-fatal: the drill still works, they just lose the resume point.
      console.error('[drill:resume:snapshot] resume write failed', {
        userId: user.id,
        key: genericResumeKey,
        err,
      });
    });
  }, [session, genericResumeKey, user, drillResumeSnapshot]);

export function drillResumeKey(input: {
  mode: string;
  config?: unknown;
  identity?: DrillResumeIdentity;
}): string | null {
  if (input.identity?.kind === 'bootcamp-day') {
    return `${DRILL_SESSION_PREFIX}bootcamp-day::${input.identity.day}::${input.identity.part}`;
  }

  const mode = input.mode;
  if (mode === 'adaptive') return `${DRILL_SESSION_PREFIX}adaptive`;

  const config = (input.config ?? null) as Record<string, unknown> | null;

  if (mode === 'full-section') {
    if (!config) return null;
    const pt = config.pt;
    const section = config.section;
    if (pt == null || section == null) return null;
    return `${DRILL_SESSION_PREFIX}section::pt${pt}-s${section}`;
  }

  if (mode === 'type-drill' || mode === 'natural-drill') {
    if (!config) return null;
    const selected = config.selectedQids as string[] | undefined;
    if (selected?.length) {
      // A pre-selected set is its own identity: the same list re-opened is the
      // same run, a different list is a different one.
      return `${DRILL_SESSION_PREFIX}${mode}::set-${fingerprint([...selected].sort().join(','))}`;
    }
    const shape = JSON.stringify({
      qtypes: config.qtypes ?? [],
      difficulties: config.difficulties ?? [],
      pts: config.pts ?? [],
      sections: config.sections ?? [],
      qfeatures: config.qfeatures ?? [],
      reasoningTypes: config.reasoningTypes ?? [],
      searchQuery: config.searchQuery ?? '',
      questionNumber: config.questionNumber ?? null,

/**
 * Write the student's current place in a practice set. Called after every
 * answer and every question move, so it must stay cheap — the persistence layer
 * writes to the local cache synchronously and debounces the Supabase upsert.
 *
 * A completed row is never downgraded back to in_progress by a snapshot write;
 * re-running a finished set starts a fresh row via resetPracticeSession.
 */
export async function savePracticeSessionSnapshot(
  userId: string,
  pool: Pick<PracticePool, 'slug' | 'completionId'>,
  snapshot: PracticeSessionSnapshot,
): Promise<void> {
  if (!userId || !pool?.slug) return;
  if (snapshot.questionQueue.length === 0) return;

  const id = practiceSessionIdFor(userId, pool.slug);
  const persist = getPersistence(userId);
  const existing = await loadPracticeSession(userId, pool.slug);

  const record = PracticeSession.parse({
    id,
    user_id: userId,
    pool_slug: pool.slug,
    completion_id: pool.completionId,
    question_queue: snapshot.questionQueue,
    current_index: Math.min(
      Math.max(snapshot.currentIndex, 0),
      Math.max(snapshot.questionQueue.length - 1, 0),
    ),
    attempts: snapshot.attempts,
    status: 'in_progress',
    // Never hand back time already spent: a snapshot with no clock keeps
    // whatever the row already knew rather than clearing it.
    elapsed_ms: snapshot.elapsedMs ?? existing?.elapsed_ms,
    regulation_answered_qids:
      snapshot.regulationAnsweredQids ?? existing?.regulation_answered_qids,
    started_at: existing?.started_at ?? now(),
    updated_at: now(),
  });

  await persist.set(TABLES.practice_sessions, id, record);
}

export interface Persistence {
  ready(): Promise<void>;

  get<T>(table: string, id: string): Promise<T | null>;
  set<T>(table: string, id: string, record: T): Promise<void>;
  remove(table: string, id: string): Promise<void>;
  list<T>(table: string, filter?: Partial<T>): Promise<T[]>;

  putBlob(key: string, blob: Blob): Promise<void>;
  getBlob(key: string): Promise<Blob | null>;
  removeBlob(key: string): Promise<void>;

  transaction<T>(fn: (tx: PersistenceTx) => Promise<T>): Promise<T>;
}

export interface PersistenceTx {
  get<T>(table: string, id: string): Promise<T | null>;
  set<T>(table: string, id: string, record: T): Promise<void>;
}
export function __setPersistenceForTests(p: Persistence | null): void {
  _testOverride = p;
  if (p === null) _cache.clear();
}

- Fixed drills: no Submit pill, no auto-reveal switch; one `commitThenNavigate` wrapper with a synchronous lock; `persistAttempt` returns a typed result; a failed save writes no state and stops the move; single-flight per question.
- Explanation shows expanders only: `ArgumentBreakdown` (chevron on a rule under the stimulus, closed by default) and a chevron at the right edge of each noted choice, one at a time, all closed on a new question. Reset Response under the choices.
